intdb {
	enum DSP_MODE {
		MULT9X9,
		MULT9X9ADDSUB,
		MULT9X9ADDSUBSUM,
		MULT18X18,
		MULT18X18MAC,
		MULT18X18ADDSUB,
		MULT18X18ADDSUBSUM,
		MULT36X36,
	}

	enum DSP_MUX_P3 {
		P,
		SRO,
	}

	bel_class SLICE {
		input A[2];
		input B[2];
		input C[2];
		input D[2];
		input M[2];
		input FXA;
		input FXB;
		input CLK;
		input LSR;
		input CE;
		output F[2];
		output Q[2];
		output OFX[2];
	}

	bel_class EBR {
		input CLKA;
		input CLKB;
		input RSTA;
		input RSTB;
		input CEA;
		input CEB;
		input WEA;
		input WEB;
		input MORCLKA;
		input MORCLKB;
		input CSA[3];
		input CSB[3];
		input ADA[14];
		input ADB[14];
		input ADB3W;
		input BLENABLE;
		input BHENABLE;
		input DIA[18];
		input DIB[18];
		output DOA[18];
		output DOB[18];
	}

	bel_class DSP {
		input CLK[4];
		input RST[4];
		input CE[4];
		input A0[18];
		input A1[18];
		input A2[18];
		input A3[18];
		input B0[18];
		input B1[18];
		input B2[18];
		input B3[18];
		input ACCUMSLOAD1;
		input ACCUMSLOAD3;
		input ADDNSUB1;
		input ADDNSUB3;
		input SIGNEDA[4];
		input SIGNEDB[4];
		input SOURCEA[4];
		input SOURCEB[4];
		input LD1[16];
		input LD3[16];
		output P0[36];
		output P1[36];
		output P2[36];
		output P3[36];
		attribute MODE: DSP_MODE;
		attribute GSR: bool;
		attribute MUX_P3: DSP_MUX_P3;
		attribute MUX_CLK_A0: bitvec[2];
		attribute MUX_RST_A0: bitvec[2];
		attribute MUX_CE_A0: bitvec[2];
		attribute REG_A0: bool;
		attribute MUX_CLK_B0: bitvec[2];
		attribute MUX_RST_B0: bitvec[2];
		attribute MUX_CE_B0: bitvec[2];
		attribute REG_B0: bool;
		attribute MUX_CLK_M0: bitvec[2];
		attribute MUX_RST_M0: bitvec[2];
		attribute MUX_CE_M0: bitvec[2];
		attribute REG_M0: bool;
		attribute MUX_CLK_P0: bitvec[2];
		attribute MUX_RST_P0: bitvec[2];
		attribute MUX_CE_P0: bitvec[2];
		attribute REG_P0: bool;
		attribute MUX_CLK_A1: bitvec[2];
		attribute MUX_RST_A1: bitvec[2];
		attribute MUX_CE_A1: bitvec[2];
		attribute REG_A1: bool;
		attribute MUX_CLK_B1: bitvec[2];
		attribute MUX_RST_B1: bitvec[2];
		attribute MUX_CE_B1: bitvec[2];
		attribute REG_B1: bool;
		attribute MUX_CLK_M1: bitvec[2];
		attribute MUX_RST_M1: bitvec[2];
		attribute MUX_CE_M1: bitvec[2];
		attribute REG_M1: bool;
		attribute MUX_CLK_P1: bitvec[2];
		attribute MUX_RST_P1: bitvec[2];
		attribute MUX_CE_P1: bitvec[2];
		attribute REG_P1: bool;
		attribute MUX_CLK_A2: bitvec[2];
		attribute MUX_RST_A2: bitvec[2];
		attribute MUX_CE_A2: bitvec[2];
		attribute REG_A2: bool;
		attribute MUX_CLK_B2: bitvec[2];
		attribute MUX_RST_B2: bitvec[2];
		attribute MUX_CE_B2: bitvec[2];
		attribute REG_B2: bool;
		attribute MUX_CLK_M2: bitvec[2];
		attribute MUX_RST_M2: bitvec[2];
		attribute MUX_CE_M2: bitvec[2];
		attribute REG_M2: bool;
		attribute MUX_CLK_P2: bitvec[2];
		attribute MUX_RST_P2: bitvec[2];
		attribute MUX_CE_P2: bitvec[2];
		attribute REG_P2: bool;
		attribute MUX_CLK_A3: bitvec[2];
		attribute MUX_RST_A3: bitvec[2];
		attribute MUX_CE_A3: bitvec[2];
		attribute REG_A3: bool;
		attribute MUX_CLK_B3: bitvec[2];
		attribute MUX_RST_B3: bitvec[2];
		attribute MUX_CE_B3: bitvec[2];
		attribute REG_B3: bool;
		attribute MUX_CLK_M3: bitvec[2];
		attribute MUX_RST_M3: bitvec[2];
		attribute MUX_CE_M3: bitvec[2];
		attribute REG_M3: bool;
		attribute MUX_CLK_P3: bitvec[2];
		attribute MUX_RST_P3: bitvec[2];
		attribute MUX_CE_P3: bitvec[2];
		attribute REG_P3: bool;

				mux IMUX_CE[2] = X0_S[0] | X0_S[1] | X0_N[0] | X0_N[1] | X2_W1[1] | X2_W2[1] | X2_E1[1] | X2_E2[1] | X2_S1[1] | X2_S2[1] | X2_N1[1] | X2_N2[1] | RSCLK[0] | RSCLK[2] | RSCLK[3];

		tile_class PLL_W {
			cell CELL[0];
			cell CELL[1];
			cell CELL[2];
			cell CELL[3];
			cell IO_CLKI[0];
			cell IO_CLKI[1];
			cell IO_CLKFB[0];
			cell IO_CLKFB[1];

			switchbox BEL_INT {
				mux CELL[0].IMUX_PLL_CLKI[0] = CELL[2].IMUX_A[0] | CELL[2].IMUX_CLK[0] | IO_CLKI[0].OUT_IO_PADDI[0] | IO_CLKI[1].OUT_IO_PADDI[0];
				mux CELL[0].IMUX_PLL_CLKFB[0] = CELL[0].ECLK[0] | CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKINTFB[0] | CELL[2].IMUX_CLK[1] | IO_CLKFB[0].OUT_IO_PADDI[0];
				mux CELL[0].IMUX_DLL_CLKI[0] = CELL[0].IMUX_A[4] | CELL[0].IMUX_CLK[0] | IO_CLKI[0].OUT_IO_PADDI[0] | IO_CLKI[1].OUT_IO_PADDI[0];
				mux CELL[0].IMUX_DLL_CLKFB[0] = CELL[0].IMUX_CLK[1] | CELL[0].ECLK[0] | CELL[0].OUT_DLL_CLKOP[0] | IO_CLKFB[1].OUT_IO_PADDI[0];
				mux CELL[0].PLL_TO_ECLK[0] = CELL[0].IMUX_CLK[0] | CELL[0].OUT_DLLDEL_CLKO[0] | CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKOP[0] | CELL[0].OUT_DLL_CLKOP[0] | CELL[0].OUT_DLLDEL_BYPASS | IO_CLKI[0].OUT_IO_PADDI[0];
				mux CELL[0].PLL_TO_ECLK[1] = CELL[0].IMUX_CLK[1] | CELL[0].OUT_DLLDEL_CLKO[0] | CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKOS[0] | CELL[0].OUT_DLL_CLKOS[0] | CELL[0].OUT_DLLDEL_BYPASS | IO_CLKI[0].OUT_IO_PADDI[0];
				mux CELL[0].IMUX_DLLDEL_CLKI = CELL[0].IMUX_A[4] | CELL[0].IMUX_CLK[0] | IO_CLKI[0].OUT_IO_PADDI[0] | IO_CLKI[1].OUT_IO_PADDI[0];
				mux CELL[0].IMUX_CLKDIV_CLKI[0] = CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKOP[0] | CELL[0].OUT_DLL_CLKOP[0] | CELL[0].PLL_TO_ECLK[0] | CELL[0].PLL_TO_ECLK[1];
			}

			bel PLL[0] {
				input CLKI = CELL[0].IMUX_PLL_CLKI[0];
				input CLKFB = CELL[0].IMUX_PLL_CLKFB[0];
				input RESETM = CELL[2].IMUX_LSR[1];
				input RESETK = CELL[2].IMUX_LSR[2];
				input CNTRST = CELL[2].IMUX_LSR[0];

				output CLKOP = CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKOP[0], CELL[2].OUT_F[5];
				output CLKOS = CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKOS[0], CELL[2].OUT_F[6];
				output CLKINTFB = CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKINTFB[0];

				output CLKOK = CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKOK, CELL[2].OUT_F[2];
				output DNLOCK = CELL[2].OUT_Q[3];
				output UPLOCK = CELL[2].OUT_Q[2];
				output DDAOZR = CELL[2].OUT_F[0];
				output DDAOLAG = CELL[2].OUT_F[4];
				output DDAODEL[0] = CELL[2].OUT_F[3];
				output DDAODEL[1] = CELL[2].OUT_F[7];
				output DDAODEL[2] = CELL[2].OUT_F[1];

			}

				mux CELL[0].IMUX_PLL_CLKI[0] = CELL[2].IMUX_A[0] | CELL[2].IMUX_CLK[0] | IO_CLKI[0].OUT_IO_PADDI[0] | IO_CLKI[1].OUT_IO_PADDI[0];
				mux CELL[0].IMUX_PLL_CLKFB[0] = CELL[0].ECLK[0] | CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKINTFB[0] | CELL[2].IMUX_CLK[1] | IO_CLKFB[0].OUT_IO_PADDI[0];
				mux CELL[0].IMUX_DLL_CLKI[0] = CELL[0].IMUX_A[4] | CELL[0].IMUX_CLK[0] | IO_CLKI[0].OUT_IO_PADDI[0] | IO_CLKI[1].OUT_IO_PADDI[0];
				mux CELL[0].IMUX_DLL_CLKFB[0] = CELL[0].IMUX_CLK[1] | CELL[0].ECLK[0] | CELL[0].OUT_DLL_CLKOP[0] | IO_CLKFB[1].OUT_IO_PADDI[0];
				mux CELL[0].PLL_TO_ECLK[0] = CELL[0].IMUX_CLK[0] | CELL[0].OUT_DLLDEL_CLKO[0] | CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKOP[0] | CELL[0].OUT_DLL_CLKOP[0] | CELL[0].OUT_DLLDEL_BYPASS | IO_CLKI[0].OUT_IO_PADDI[0];
				mux CELL[0].PLL_TO_ECLK[1] = CELL[0].IMUX_CLK[1] | CELL[0].OUT_DLLDEL_CLKO[0] | CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKOS[0] | CELL[0].OUT_DLL_CLKOS[0] | CELL[0].OUT_DLLDEL_BYPASS | IO_CLKI[0].OUT_IO_PADDI[0];
				mux CELL[0].IMUX_DLLDEL_CLKI = CELL[0].IMUX_A[4] | CELL[0].IMUX_CLK[0] | IO_CLKI[0].OUT_IO_PADDI[0] | IO_CLKI[1].OUT_IO_PADDI[0];
				mux CELL[0].IMUX_CLKDIV_CLKI[0] = CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKOP[0] | CELL[0].OUT_DLL_CLKOP[0] | CELL[0].PLL_TO_ECLK[0] | CELL[0].PLL_TO_ECLK[1];
			}

			bel PLL[0] {

			reflect X2_S1[1] = X2_N0[1];
			reflect X2_S1[2] = X2_N0[2];
			reflect X2_S1[3] = X2_N0[3];
			reflect X2_S1[4] = X2_N0[4];
			reflect X2_S1[5] = X2_N0[5];
			reflect X2_S1[6] = X2_N0[6];
			reflect X2_S1[7] = X2_N0[7];
			reflect X2_S2[0] = X2_N1[0];
			reflect X2_S2[1] = X2_N1[1];
			reflect X2_S2[2] = X2_N1[2];
			reflect X2_S2[3] = X2_N1[3];
			reflect X2_S2[4] = X2_N1[4];
			reflect X2_S2[5] = X2_N1[5];
			reflect X2_S2[6] = X2_N1[6];
			reflect X2_S2[7] = X2_N1[7];
			reflect X6_S1[0] = X6_N0[0];
			reflect X6_S1[1] = X6_N0[1];
			reflect X6_S1[2] = X6_N0[2];
			reflect X6_S1[3] = X6_N0[3];
			reflect X6_S2[0] = X6_N1[0];
			reflect X6_S2[1] = X6_N1[1];
			reflect X6_S2[2] = X6_N1[2];
			reflect X6_S2[3] = X6_N1[3];
			reflect X6_S3[0] = X6_N2[0];
			reflect X6_S3[1] = X6_N2[1];
			reflect X6_S3[2] = X6_N2[2];
			reflect X6_S3[3] = X6_N2[3];
			reflect X6_S4[0] = X6_N3[0];
			reflect X6_S4[1] = X6_N3[1];
			reflect X6_S4[2] = X6_N3[2];
			reflect X6_S4[3] = X6_N3[3];
			reflect X6_S5[0] = X6_N4[0];
			reflect X6_S5[1] = X6_N4[1];
			reflect X6_S5[2] = X6_N4[2];
			reflect X6_S5[3] = X6_N4[3];
			reflect X6_S6[0] = X6_N5[0];
			reflect X6_S6[1] = X6_N5[1];
			reflect X6_S6[2] = X6_N5[2];
			reflect X6_S6[3] = X6_N5[3];
		}
	}

	connector_slot RSCLK_W {
		opposite RSCLK_E;

		connector_class PASS_SW {
			pass RSCLK_H[0] = RSCLK_H[1];
			pass RSCLK_H[1] = RSCLK_H[2];
			pass RSCLK_H[2] = RSCLK_H[3];
			pass RSCLK_H[3] = RSCLK_H[0];
			pass RSCLK_H[4] = RSCLK_H[5];
			pass RSCLK_H[5] = RSCLK_H[6];
			pass RSCLK_H[6] = RSCLK_H[7];
			pass RSCLK_H[7] = RSCLK_H[4];
		}
	}

	connector_slot RSCLK_E {
		opposite RSCLK_W;

		connector_class PASS_SE {
		}
	}
